[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
//                      Baseline JPEG Decoder
//                             V0.1
//                       Ultra-Embedded.com
//                        Copyright 2020
//
//                   admin@ultra-embedded.com
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
//                      License: Apache 2.0

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;

reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;

reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]

    end
end

    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

[ASSISTANT]
Baseline passes with cost 9.50M (area 4273, delay 2222ps — critical path is the transpose RAM read mux). Now let me write an optimized version with several targeted improvements:

1. **Width reduction**: x-pass outputs fit in 21-bit signed `(t>>>11 ∈ [-2^20,2^20))` → transpose RAMs store 21 bits instead of 32; y-pass outputs fit 17 bits.
2. **Narrow multipliers**: 17×13 (x-pass) and 22×13 (y-pass) instead of 32×32.
3. **Remove redundant product register stage** (re-scheduled).
4. **`*181` as shift-adds, `/256` truncating-toward-zero as floor-shift + conditional increment** (avoids a divider).
5. **`ptr_conv` mux → simple bit concatenation** `{ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}`.

[ASSISTANT]
The transpose RAM read mux dominates delay. Let me try two structural fixes: code the RAM like the original (separate read/write always blocks) and add a pipeline stage at the y-pass input:
  -> create_file(content="//=====================================================================\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg algorithm, exact fixed\n// point) — width/resource optimized version.\n//\n// Exactness arguments:\n//  * x-pass outputs are (32-bit wrapped t) >>> 11, hence always in\n//    [-2^20, 2^20)  -> stored/sign-extended with 21 bits, bit-exact.\n//  * x-pass multiplier operands fit 17 bits (coeffs 16b, i0 = 17b),\n//    constants fit 13 bits signed -> products fit 30 bits, no wrap,\n//    sign-extension into the 32-bit accumulator context is exact.\n//  * y-pass inputs fit 21 bits (i0 fits 22); products may exceed 32\n//    bits, so the 35-bit true product is truncated to 32 bits (wrap),\n//    matching the original 32x32 wrap multiply [ASSISTANT]
Excellent progress — correctness maintained and area down. Now I have a key structural insight: by deriving the exact transpose read schedule, the y-pass at beat (row r, beat j) consumes exactly bank r of the x-output block (slots {2m+o}), where o = j[0]^j[2]. This lets me replace the four 32-entry RAMs (with their huge 32:1 read muxes) by 8 banks of 16 entries, read via a per-row latch — the read path becomes tiny 2:1 muxes. Let me build design4:
  -> create_file(content='//=====================================================================\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg algorithm, exact fixed\n// point) — width + structure optimized version.\n//\n// Exactness arguments:\n//  * x-pass outputs are (32-bit wrapped t) >>> 11, hence always in\n//    [-2^20, 2^20)  -> stored/sign-extended with 21 bits, bit-exact.\n//  * x-pass multiplier operands fit 17 bits (coeffs 16b, i0 = 17b),\n//    constants fit 13 bits signed -> products fit 30 bits, no wrap.\n//  * y-pass inputs fit 21 bits (i0 fits 22); products may exceed 32\n//    bits, so the 35-bit true product is truncated to 32 bits (wrap),\n//    matching the original 32x32 wrap multiply exactly.\n//  * x*181 is computed as shift-adds keeping only the low 32 bits\n//    (identical to the original 32-bit wrap multiply); /256 is signed\n//    division truncating toward zero = floor shift + conditional inc.\n//\n// Corner turn restructure:\n//  The original corner turn stores the x-pass block into 4 RAMs and\n//  re-reads it with a walking address.  Working out that schedule, the\n//  y-pass at output row r, beat j consumes exactly the x-pass outputs\n//  x[r + 8*(2m+o)] on lane m, where o = j[0]^j[2]  (o=0 for the even\n//  coefficient beats j in {0,2,5,7}, o=1 for {1,3,4,6}).  I.e. it\n//  consumes column r of the x-output block = "bank r" when the block\n//  is stored bank = idx[2:0], slot = idx[5:3].  So: 8 banks of 16\n//  entries (2 block parities x 8 slots); a row latch grabs the whole\n//  bank at the start of each y row and the per-beat lane muxes are\n//  just 2:1s — no 32:1 address decode anywhere on the read path.\n//=====================================================================\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input wire [15:0] inport_data2_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data3_i,\n     input  wire [ 2:0] inport_idx_i,\n     output wire        outport_valid_o,\n     output wire [31:0] outport_data_o,\n     output wire [ 5:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nwire          x_valid_w;\nwire [20:0]   x_data_w;\nwire [ 5:0]   x_idx_w;\nwire          x_accept_w;\n\nwire          tr_valid_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data0_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data1_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data2_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data3_w;\nwire [ 2:0]   tr_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_x u_idct_x (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(x_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data_o(x_data_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(x_idx_w)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_transpose u_transpose (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(x_valid_w)\n ,.outport_data2_o(tr_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(tr_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(tr_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1\'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y u_idct_y (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(tr_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(tr_data0_w)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(tr_data1_w)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(tr_data2_w)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(tr_data3_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(tr_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(outport_valid_o)\n    ,.outport_data_o(outport_data_o)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// 1-D IDCT row pass (OUT_SHIFT = [ASSISTANT]
Big win: cost 4.69M (baseline 9.50M). Critical path is now the y-pass 22×13 multiplier (~940ps). Let me pipeline the multipliers into two stages (split b into low-8/high-5 partial products), shifting the downstream beat schedule by one cycle:
  -> create_file(content="//=====================================================================\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg algorithm, exact fixed\n// point) — width + structure optimized version, 2-stage multipliers.\n//\n// Exactness arguments:\n//  * x-pass outputs are (32-bit wrapped t) >>> 11, hence always in\n//    [-2^20, 2^20)  -> stored/sign-extended with 21 bits, bit-exact.\n//  * x-pass multiplier operands fit 17 bits (coeffs 16b, i0 = 17b),\n//    constants fit 13 bits signed -> products fit 30 bits, no wrap.\n//  * y-pass inputs fit 21 bits (i0 fits 22); products may exceed 32\n//    bits, so the true 35-bit product is truncated to 32 bits (wrap),\n//    matching the original 32x32 wrap multiply exactly.\n//  * The 13-bit constant multiply is pipelined: b = bh*256 + bl with\n//    bl = b[7:0] unsigned, bh = b[12:8] signed 5-bit; the exact sum\n//    (bh*a)<<8 + bl*a is formed in stage 2 — bit-identical to a*b.\n//  * x*181 is computed as shift-adds keeping only the low 32 bits\n//    (identical to the original 32-bit wrap multiply); /256 is signed\n//    division truncating toward zero = floor shift + conditional inc.\n//\n// Corner turn restructure:\n//  The y-pass at output row r, beat j consumes exactly the x-pass\n//  outputs x[r + 8*(2m+o)] on lane m, where o = j[0]^j[2].  So: 8\n//  banks of 16 entries (2 block parities x 8 slots); a row latch\n//  grabs the whole bank at the start of each y row and the per-beat\n//  lane muxes are just 2:1s — no 32:1 address decode anywhere.\n//=====================================================================\n\n// 2-stage pipelined signed multiply by a 13-bit signed constant:\n//   stage 1: partial products a*bl (bl = b[7:0] unsigned),\n//            a*bh (bh = b[12:8] signed)\n//   stage 2: sum, low 32 bits (wrap semantics of a 32-bit multiply)\nmodule idct_mul13 #(\n    parameter AW = 22\n) (\n    input  wire                    clk,\n    input  wire                    rst,\n    input  wire signed [AW-1:0]    a_i,\n    input  wire signed [12:0]      b_i,\n    output wire signed [31:0]      p_o\n);\n    wire signed [AW+8:0] lo_w = $signed(a_i) * $signed({1'b0, b_i[7:0]});\n    wire signed [AW+4:0] hi_w = $signed(a_i) * $signed(b_i[12:8]);\n\n    reg signed [AW+8:0] lo_q;\n    reg signed [AW+4:0] hi_q;\n\n    always @(posedge clk)\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        lo_q <= 0;\n        hi_q <= 0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        lo_q <= lo_w;\n        hi_q <= hi_w;\n    end\n\n    wire signed [AW+12:0] full = (hi_q <<< 8) + lo_q;\n    assign p_o = full[31:0];\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data0_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data1_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data2_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data3_i,\n     input  wire [ 2:0] inport_idx_i,\n     output wire        outport_valid_o,\n     output wire [31:0] outport_data_o,\n     output wire [ 5:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nwire          x_valid_w;\nwire [20:0]   x_data_w;\nwire [ 5:0]   x_idx_w;\nwire          x_accept_w;\n\nwire          tr_valid_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data0_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data1_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data2_w;\nwire [20:0]   tr_data3_w;\nwire [ 2:0]   tr_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_x u_idct_x (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(x_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data_o(x_data_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(x_idx_w)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_transpose u_transpose (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(x_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data_i(x_data_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(x_idx_w)\n    ,.inport_accept_o(x_accept_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(tr_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data0_o(tr_data0_w)\n    ,.outport_data1_o(tr_data1_w)\n    ,.outport_data2_o(tr_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(tr_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(tr_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y u_idct_y (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(tr_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(tr_data0_w)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(tr_data1_w)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(tr_data2_w)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(tr_data3_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(tr_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(outport_valid_o)\n    ,.outport_data_o(outport_data_o)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// 1-D IDCT row pass (OUT_SHIFT = 11), pipelined multipliers\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_x (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data0_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data1_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] 5:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [16:0] block_in_0_1 = signed [16:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b;\nreg signed [12:0] mul1_b;\nreg signed [12:0] mul2_b;\nreg signed [12:0] mul3_b;\nreg signed [12:0] mul4_b;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0     <= 17'sd0;\n    mul0_a <= 17'sd0; mul0_b <= 13'sd0;\n    mul1_a <= 17'sd0; mul1_b <= 13'sd0;\n    mul2_a <= 17'sd0; mul2_b <= 13'sd0;\n    mul3_a <= 17'sd0; mul3_b <= 13'sd0;\n    mul4_a <= 17'sd0; mul4_b <= 13'sd0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nwire signed [31:0] mul0;\nwire signed [31:0] mul1;\nwire signed [31:0] mul2;\nwire signed [31:0] mul3;\nwire signed [31:0] mul4;\n\nidct_mul13 #(.AW(17)) u_mul0 (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .a_i(mul0_a), .b_i(mul0_b), .p_o(mul0));\nidct_mul13 #(.AW(17)) u_mul1 (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .a_i(mul1_a), .b_i(mul1_b), .p_o(mul1));\nidct_mul13 #(.AW(17)) u_mul2 (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .a_i(mul2_a), .b_i(mul2_b), .p_o(mul2));\nidct_mul13 #(.AW(17)) u_mul3 (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .a_i(mul3_a), .b_i(mul3_b), .p_o(mul3));\nidct_mul13 #(.AW(17)) u_mul4 (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .a_i(mul4_a), .b_i(mul4_b), .p_o(mul4));\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;\n    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;\n    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;\n    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;\n    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// x*181 keeping low 32 bits (identical to 32-bit wrap multiply)\nfunction [31:0] mul181_wrap;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        mul181_wrap = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5) + (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2) + x;\n    end\nendfunction\n\n// signed division by 256 truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [31:0] div256_trunc;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] f;\n    begin\n        f = x >>> 8;\n        if (x[31] && (x[7:0] != 8'h00))\n            f = f + 32'sd1;\n        div256_trunc = f;\n    end\nendfunction\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t1;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t2;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t3;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t4;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_s6   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_s7   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t0   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t1   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t2   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t3   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t4   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t5   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t6   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t7   <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'sd0;\n    o_t5_6 <= 32'sd0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;\n        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;\n        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4\n        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2\n        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1\n        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd6:\n    begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default:\n    begin\n        o_s5 <= div256_trunc(mul181_wrap(o_t6_5)); // 1/sqrt(2)\n        o_s6 <= div256_trunc(mul181_wrap(o_t5_6)); // 1/sqrt(2)\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\n// results of (32-bit >>> 11) always fit in 21 signed bits\nreg signed [20:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [20:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[1] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[2] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[3] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[4] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[5] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[6] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out[7] <= 21'sd0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 21'sd0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> 11);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> 11); // block_out[7]\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> 11);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> 11);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> 11);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> 11);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> 11);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> 11);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q  <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q  <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Double-buffered corner turn, banked (see header note)\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [20:0] inport_data_i,\n     input  wire [ 5:0] inport_idx_i,\n     input  wire        outport_ready_i,\n     output wire        inport_accept_o,\n     output wire        outport_valid_o,\n     output wire [20:0] outport_data0_o,\n     output wire [20:0] outport_data1_o,\n     output wire [20:0] outport_data2_o,\n     output wire [20:0] outport_data3_o,\n     output wire [ 2:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [1:0]   block_ready_q;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Storage: 8 banks x (2 parities x 8 slots), 21-bit entries.\n// x-out beat k writes bank k[2:0], slot k[5:3], parity block_wr_q.\n// y row r reads bank r (slots 0..7) of parity block_rd_q.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [20:0] stor [0:127];    // index {bank[2:0], parity, slot[2:0]}\n\nwire       wen    = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o;\nwire [6:0] waddr  = {inport_idx_i[2:0], block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[5:3]};\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\n    if (wen)\n        stor[waddr] <= inport_data_i;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Row latch read path\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire [2:0] r_r    = rd_idx_q[5:3];\nwire [2:0] j_r    = rd_idx_q[2:0];\nwire       cap    = (rd_idx_q != 6'd63) && (j_r == 3'd7);\n// at idx 63: block_rd_q toggles, so load row 0 of the parity AFTER toggle;\n// at idx 0 (first ever row): load row 0 of current parity only if a block\n// has actually been received (block_ready_q != 0).\nwire       cap0   = (rd_idx_q == 6'd0) && (block_ready_q != 2'd0);\nwire [2:0] cap_r  = cap ? (r_r + 3'd1) : 3'd0;\n\nreg [20:0] row_latch [0:7];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (cap || cap0)\nbegin\n    row_latch[0] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd0}];\n    row_latch[1] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd1}];\n    row_latch[2] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd2}];\n    row_latch[3] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd3}];\n    row_latch[4] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd4}];\n    row_latch[5] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd5}];\n    row_latch[6] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd6}];\n    row_latch[7] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd7}];\nend\n\n// o = j[0] ^ j[2]  (1 for j in {1,3,4,6})\nwire       o_w    = j_r[0] ^ j_r[2];\n\nassign outport_data0_o = o_w ? row_latch[1] : row_latch[0];\nassign outport_data1_o = o_w ? row_latch[3] : row_latch[2];\nassign outport_data2_o = o_w ? row_latch[5] : row_latch[4];\nassign outport_data3_o = o_w ? row_latch[7] : row_latch[6];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Input Buffer\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nalways @(posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// FSM\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n    begin\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    end\n    STATE_SETUP:\n    begin\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    end\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @(posedge clk )\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @(posedge clk )\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// 1-D IDCT column pass (OUT_SHIFT = 15), pipelined multipliers\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_y (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [20:0] inport_data0_i,\n     input  wire [20:0] inport_data1_i,\n     input  wire [20:0] inport_data2_i,\n     input  wire [20:0] inport_data3_i,\n     input  wire [ 2:0] inport_idx_i,\n     output wire        outport_valid_o,\n     output wire [31:0] outport_data_o,\n     output wire [ 5:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\n// x-pass outputs always fit in 21 signed bits\nwire signed [20:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [20:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [20:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [20:0] block_in_6_7 = [ASSISTANT]
The empirical evidence shows 32-bit adds are ~350ps but the multiplier CPA is the bottleneck. My 2-stage split kept full CPAs in *both* stages — wrong split. Better plan: stage 1 = AND rows + carry-save tree → register **(sum,carry)** vectors (no CPA); stage 2 = one 32-bit add. Since all constants are positive (<4096) and using a biased-operand trick with mod-2^32 arithmetic (which exactly replicates the original 32-bit wrap multiply), this is bit-exact. Also, I proved x-pass outputs fit in 20 bits (bounding all sums < 2^30), shrinking the transpose. Let me write design6:
  -> create_file(content="//=====================================================================\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg algorithm, exact fixed\n// point) — width + structure optimized, 2-stage carry-save multipliers.\n//\n// Exactness arguments:\n//  * x-pass outputs are (32-bit wrapped t) >>> 11.  All x-pass\n//    intermediates are bounded by 2^30 in magnitude (max |t| =\n//    2^15*(4017+3784+3406+2896) + 2^15*(4017+799+2276+1567) < 2^30),\n//    so no wrap occurs and the results fit in 20 signed bits\n//    [-2^19, 2^19).  Stored/extended with 20 bits — bit-exact.\n//  * x-pass multiplier operands fit 17 bits, constants fit 13 bits.\n//  * y-pass inputs fit 20 bits (i0 fits 21).  Products may exceed 32\n//    bits, so all product arithmetic is done mod 2^32, matching the\n//    original 32x32 wrap multiply exactly (the carry-save tree and the\n//    final 32-bit add both operate mod 2^32; dropped carry-outs ARE\n//    the wrap).\n//  * The multiply a*C (C > 0, registered) uses the biased-operand\n//    identity a*C = a_u*C - 2^(AW-1)*C with a_u = a + 2^(AW-1)\n//    (unsigned).  Rows: a_u gated by C[k], shifted by k, plus one\n//    constant correction row C<<(AW-1).  Reduced by a 3:2 CSA tree to\n//    (sum, carry), registered, then summed mod 2^32.\n//  * x*181 is computed as shift-adds keeping only the low 32 bits\n//    (identical to the original 32-bit wrap multiply); /256 is signed\n//    division truncating toward zero = floor shift + conditional inc.\n//\n// Corner turn restructure:\n//  The y-pass at output row r, beat j consumes exactly the x-pass\n//  outputs x[r + 8*(2m+o)] on lane m, where o = j[0]^j[2].  So: 8\n//  banks of 16 entries (2 block parities x 8 slots); a row latch\n//  grabs the whole bank at the start of each y row and the per-beat\n//  lane muxes are just 2:1s — no 32:1 address decode anywhere.\n//=====================================================================\n\n// 2-stage pipelined multiply by a positive 13-bit constant (bit-exact\n// 32-bit wrap semantics):\n//   stage 1: biased-operand partial rows -> CSA tree -> (sum,carry) reg\n//   stage 2: 32-bit wrap add of the two vectors\nmodule idct_mul13 #(\n    parameter AW = 21\n) (\n    input  wire                   clk,\n    input  wire                   rst,\n    input  wire signed [AW-1:0]   a_i,\n    input  wire [12:0]            b_i,   // positive constant < 4096\n    output reg  [31:0]            p_o\n);\n    // biased unsigned operand: a_u = a + 2^(AW-1)  (just flips the MSB)\n    wire [AW-1:0] a_u = {~a_i[AW-1], a_i[AW-2:0]};\n\n    // partial rows, all mod 2^32 (32-bit wrap == original semantics)\n    wire [31:0] r [0:12];\n    genvar k;\n    generate\n        for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1) begin : ROWS\n            assign r[k] = (a_u & {AW{b_i[k]}}) << k;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    // correction row: -2^(AW-1)*C  mod 2^32\n    assign r[12] = b_i << (AW-1);\n\n    // 3:2 carry-save reducers (all mod 2^32)\n    function [31:0] csa_s;\n        input [31:0] x, y, z;\n        begin\n            csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function [31:0] csa_c;\n        input [31:0] x, y, z;\n        begin\n            csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    // 13 rows -> 2 vectors, 5 CSA levels\n    wire [31:0] a0 = csa_s(r[0],  r[1],  r[2]);\n    wire [31:0] b0 = csa_c(r[0],  r[1],  r[2]);\n    wire [31:0] a1 = csa_s(r[3],  r[4],  r[5]);\n    wire [31:0] b1 = csa_c(r[3],  r[4],  r[5]);\n    wire [31:0] a2 = csa_s(r[6],  r[7],  r[8]);\n    wire [31:0] b2 = csa_c(r[6],  r[7],  r[8]);\n    wire [31:0] a3 = csa_s(r[9],  r[10], r[11]);\n    wire [31:0] b3 = csa_c(r[9],  r[10], r[11]);\n\n    wire [31:0] c0 = csa_s(a0, b0, a1);\n    wire [31:0] d0 = csa_c(a0, b0, a1);\n    wire [31:0] c1 = csa_s(b1, a2, b2);\n    wire [31:0] d1 = csa_c(b1, a2, b2);\n    wire [31:0] c2 = csa_s(a3, b3, r[12]);\n    wire [31:0] d2 = csa_c(a3, b3, r[12]);\n\n    wire [31:0] e0 = csa_s(c0, d0, c1);\n    wire [31:0] f0 = csa_c(c0, d0, c1);\n    wire [31:0] e1 = csa_s(d1, c2, d2);\n    wire [31:0] f1 = csa_c(d1, c2, d2);\n\n    wire [31:0] g0 = csa_s(e0, f0, e1);\n    wire [31:0] h0 = csa_c(e0, f0, e1);\n\n    wire [31:0] s_w = csa_s(g0, h0, f1);\n    wire [31:0] c_w = csa_c(g0, h0, f1);\n\n    reg [31:0] s_q;\n    reg [31:0] c_q;\n\n    always @(posedge clk)\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        s_q <= 32'b0;\n        c_q <= 32'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        s_q <= s_w;\n        c_q <= c_w;\n    end\n\n    always @(posedge clk)\n    if (rst)\n        p_o <= 32'b0;\n    else\n        p_o <= s_q + (c_q << 1);\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data0_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data1_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data2_i,\n     input  wire [15:0] inport_data3_i,\n     input  wire [ 2:0] inport_idx_i,\n     output wire        outport_valid_o,\n     output wire [31:0] outport_data_o,\n     output wire [ 5:0] outport_idx_o\n);\n\nwire          x_valid_w;\nwire [19:0]   x_data_w;\nwire [ 5:0]   x_idx_w;\nwire          x_accept_w;\n\nwire          tr_valid_w;\nwire [19:0]   tr_data0_w;\nwire [19:0]   tr_data1_w;\nwire [19:0]   tr_data2_w;\nwire [19:0]   tr_data3_w;\nwire [ 2:0]   tr_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_x u_idct_x (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(x_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data_o(x_data_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(x_idx_w)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_transpose u_transpose (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(x_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data_i(x_data_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(x_idx_w)\n    ,.inport_accept_o(x_accept_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(tr_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data0_o(tr_data0_w)\n    ,.outport_data1_o(tr_data1_w)\n    ,.outport_data2_o(tr_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(tr_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(tr_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y u_idct_y (\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(tr_valid_w)\n ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// 1-D IDCT row pass (OUT_SHIFT = [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        mul181_wrap = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5) + (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2) + x;\n    end\nendfunction\n\n// signed division by 256 truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [31:0] div256_trunc;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] f;\n    begin\n        f = x >>> 8;\n        if (x[31] && (x[7:0] != 8'h00))\n            f = f + 32'sd1;\n        div256_trunc = f;\n div256_trunc(mul181_wrap(o_t6_5)); // 1/sqrt(2)\n        o_s6 <= div256_trunc(mul181_wrap(o_t5_6)); // 1/sqrt(2)\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\n// results of (32-bit >>> 11) always fit in 20 signed bits\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 20'sd0;\n    block_out[1] <= 20'sd0;\n    block_out[2] <= (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> 11);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> 11); // block_out[7]\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> 11);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> 11);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Double-buffered corner turn, banked (see header note)\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire        rst,\n     input  wire        img_start_i,\n     input  wire        img_end_i,\n     input  wire        inport_valid_i,\n     input  wire [19:0] inport_data_i,\n     input  wire [ 5:0] inport_idx_i,\n     input  wire parity block_wr_q.\n// y row r reads bank r (slots 0..7) of parity block_rd_q.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [19:0] stor [0:127];    // index {bank[2:0], stor[waddr] <= inport_data_i;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Row latch read path\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire [2:0] r_r    = rd_idx_q[5:3];\nwire [2:0] j_r    = rd_idx_q[2:0];\nwire       cap    = (rd_idx_q != 6'd63) && (j_r == 3'd7);\n// at idx 63: block_rd_q toggles, so load row 0 of the parity AFTER toggle;\n// at idx 0 (first ever row): load row 0 of current parity only if a block\n// has actually been received (block_ready_q != 0).\nwire       cap0   = (rd_idx_q == 6'd0) && (block_ready_q != 2'd0);\nwire [2:0] cap_r  = cap ? (r_r + 3'd1) : 3'd0;\n\nreg [19:0] row_latch [0:7];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (cap || cap0)\nbegin\n    row_latch[0] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd0}];\n    row_latch[1] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd1}];\n    row_latch[2] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd2}];\n    row_latch[3] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd6}];\n    row_latch[7] <= stor[{cap_r, block_rd_q ^ (rd_idx_q == 6'd63), 3'd7}];\nend\n\n// o = j[0] ^ j[2]  (1 for j in {1,3,4,6})\nwire       o_w    = j_r[0] ^ outport_data3_o = o_w ? row_latch[7] : row_latch[6];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Input Buffer\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nalways @(posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// FSM\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n    begin\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    end\n outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// 1-D IDCT column pass The bug is the correction row's **sign**: I added `C<<(AW-1)` but the biased-operand identity needs to **subtract** it. In mod-2^32 arithmetic, the correction row is `-（C<<(AW-1)) mod 2^32` (a positive value = two's complement), which my CSA tree then sums correctly:
  -> edit_file(filename='design6.v', new_string="    // correction row: -(C<<(AW-1)) mod 2^32  (a positive 32-bit value:\n    // the two's complement), making the total == a*C mod 2^32\n    wire [31:0] c32 = {19'd0, b_i};\n    assign r[12] = -(c32 << (AW-1));", old_string='    // correction row: -2^(AW-1)*C  mod 2^32\n    assign r[12] = b_i << (AW-1);')
