[ASSISTANT]
Excellent: **4.26M** (delay 992ps, area 4295). The critical path is now the `*181/256` path (5-term add + shift + conditional increment, all in one cycle). There's a spare cycle in the schedule (the `o_s5/o_s6` value is computed one beat before it's consumed), so I'll restructure it: beat A computes the raw wrapped product `m = 181*x mod 2^32` via an explicit CSA tree + register, beat B folds the `>>>8`/truncation into the existing block_out adder as a carry-in:
  -> create_file(content="//=====================================================================\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg algorithm, exact fixed\n// point) — width + structure optimized, carry-save multipliers and a\n// 2-cycle *181/256 path.\n//\n// Exactness arguments:\n//  * x-pass outputs are (32-bit wrapped t) >>> 11.  All x-pass\n//    intermediates are bounded by 2^30 in magnitude (max |t| =\n//    2^15*(4017+3784+3406+2896) + 2^15*(4017+799+2276+1567) < 2^30),\n//    so no wrap occurs and the results fit in 20 signed bits\n//    [-2^19, 2^19).  Stored/extended with 20 bits — bit-exact.\n//  * x-pass multiplier operands fit 17 bits, constants fit 13 bits.\n//  * y-pass inputs fit 20 bits (i0 fits 21).  Products may exceed 32\n//    bits, so all product arithmetic is done mod 2^32, matching the\n//    original 32x32 wrap multiply exactly (the carry-save tree and the\n//    final 32-bit add both operate mod 2^32; dropped carry-outs ARE\n//    the wrap).\n//  * The multiply a*C (C > 0, registered) uses the biased-operand\n//    identity a*C = a_u*C - 2^(AW-1)*C with a_u = a + 2^(AW-1)\n//    (unsigned).  Rows: a_u gated by C[k], shifted by k, plus one\n//    constant correction row -(C<<(AW-1)) mod 2^32.  Reduced by a 3:2\n//    CSA tree to (sum, carry), registered, then summed mod 2^32.\n//  * x*181 mod 2^32 = CSA reduction of {x, x<<2, x<<4, x<<5, x<<7}\n//    plus one 32-bit wrap add (identical to the original expression).\n//    /256 truncating toward zero = arithmetic shift by 8 plus a\n//    conditional increment for negative non-exact values; the\n//    increment is folded into the carry-in of the following adder.\n//  * Schedule note: the scaled values are registered one beat before\n//    the block_out write that consumes them, so the *181 add and the\n//    final truncating add live in different cycles.\n//\n// Corner turn restructure:\n//  The y-pass at output row r, beat j consumes exactly the x-pass\n//  outputs x[r + 8*(2m+o)] on lane m, where o = j[0]^j[2].  So: 8\n//  banks of 16 entries (2 block parities x 8 slots); a row latch\n//  grabs the whole bank at the start of each y row and the per-beat\n//  lane muxes are just 2:1s — no 32:1 address decode anywhere.\n//=====================================================================\n\n// 2-stage pipelined multiply by a positive 13-bit constant (bit-exact\n// 32-bit wrap semantics):\n//   stage 1: biased-operand partial rows -> CSA tree -> (sum,carry) reg\n//   stage 2: 32-bit wrap add of the two vectors\nmodule idct_mul13 #(\n    parameter AW = 21\n) (\n    input  wire                   clk,\n    input  wire                   rst,\n    input  wire signed [AW-1:0]   a_i,\n    input  wire [12:0]            b_i,   // positive constant < 4096\n    output reg  [31:0]            p_o\n);\n    // biased unsigned operand: a_u = a + 2^(AW-1)  (just flips the MSB)\n    wire [AW-1:0] a_u = {~a_i[AW-1], a_i[AW-2:0]};\n\n    // partial rows, all mod 2^32 (32-bit wrap == original semantics)\n    wire [31:0] r [0:12];\n    genvar k;\n    generate\n        for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1) begin : ROWS\n            assign r[k] = (a_u & {AW{b_i[k]}}) << k;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    // correction row: -(C<<(AW-1)) mod 2^32  (a positive 32-bit value:\n    // the two's complement), making the total == a*C mod 2^32\n    wire [31:0] c32 = {19'd0, b_i};\n    assign r[12] = -(c32 << (AW-1));\n\n    // 3:2 carry-save reducers (all mod 2^32)\n    function [31:0] csa_s;\n        input [31:0] x, y, z;\n        begin\n            csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function [31:0] csa_c;\n        input [31:0] x, y, z;\n        begin\n            csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n\n    // 13 rows -> 2 vectors, 5 CSA levels\n    wire [31:0] a0 = csa_s(r[0],  r[1],  r[2]);\n    wire [31:0] b0 = csa_c(r[0],  r[1],  r[2]);\n    wire [31:0] a1 = csa_s(r[3],  r[4],  r[5]);\n    wire [31:0] b1 = csa_c(r[3],  r[4],  r[5]);\n    wire [31:0] a2 = csa_s(r[6],  r[7],  r[8]);\n    wire [31:0] b2 = csa_c(r[6],  r[7],  r[8]);\n    wire [31:0] a3 = csa_s(r[9],  r[10], r[11]);\n    wire [31:0] b3 = csa_c(r[9],  r[10], r[11]);\n\n    wire [31:0] c0 = csa_s(a0, b0, a1);\n    wire [31:0] d0 = csa_c(a0, b0, a1);\n    wire [31:0] c1 = csa_s(b1, a2, b2);\n    wire [31:0] d1 = csa_c(b1, a2, b2);\n    wire [31:0] c2 = csa_s(a3, b3, r[12]);\n    wire [31:0] d2 = csa_c(a3, b3, r[12]);\n\n    wire [31:0] e0 = csa_s(c0, d0, c1);\n    wire [31:0] f0 = csa_c(c0, d0, c1);\n    wire [31:0] e1 = csa_s(d1, c2, d2);\n    wire [31:0] f1 = csa_c(d1, c2, d2);\n\n    wire [31:0] g0 = csa_s(e0, f0, e1);\n    wire [31:0] h0 = csa_c(e0, f0, e1);\n\n    wire [31:0] s_w = csa_s(g0, h0, f1);\n    wire [31:0] c_w = csa_c(g0, h0, f1);\n\n    reg [31:0] s_q;\n    reg [31:0] c_q;\n\n    always @(posedge clk)\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        s_q <= 32'b0;\n        c_q <= 32'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        s_q <= s_w;\n        c_q <= c_w;\n    end\n\n    always @(posedge clk)\n    if (rst)\n        p_o <= 32'b0;\n    else\n        p_o <= s_q + c_q;   // c_q already carries the 2x weight\nendmodule\n\n// combinational: m = 181*x mod 2^32 (wrap, as in the original), plus\n// the /256 truncate-toward-zero correction bit\nmodule idct_scale181 (\n    input  wire signed [31:0] x,\n    output wire        [31:0] m,\n    output wire               incr\n);\n    wire [31:0] r0 = x;\n    wire [31:0] r1 = x << 2;\n    wire [31:0] r2 = x << 4;\n    wire [31:0] r3 = x << 5;\n    wire [31:0] r4 = x << 7;\n\n    wire [31:0] s1 = r0 ^ r1 ^ r2;\n    wire [31:0] c1 = ((r0 & r1) | (r0 & r2) | (r1 & r2)) << 1;\n    wire [31:0] s2 = r3 ^ r4;\n    wire [31:0] c2 = (r3 & r4) << 1;\n\n    wire [31:0] s3 = s1 ^ c1 ^ s2;\n    wire [31:0] c3 = ((s1 & c1) | (s1 & s2) | (c1 & s2)) << 1;\n\n    wire [31:0] s4 = c3 ^ c2;\n    wire [31:0] c4 = (c3 & c2) << 1;\n\n    assign m = s4 + c4;\n    // signed division by 256 truncating toward zero:\n    // (m>>>8) + (m<0 && (m & 255) != 0)\n    assign incr = m[31] & (m[7:0] != 8'b0);\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d (\n     input  wire        clk,\n     input  wire ## Summary

**Final result:** `design7.v` — PASS, cost **3.87M** area_delay_product (baseline starting point: 9.50M, ~2.45× better).

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Baseline** (`design.v` = verbatim starting point): cost 9.50M (area 4274, delay 2222ps; critical path = transpose RAM read mux).
- **Width reduction + RTL restructuring** (`design2`/`design3`): narrowed multipliers (17×13 / 22×13), 21-bit transpose datapath, removed a redundant pipeline stage, replaced the `ptr_conv` LUT mux with `{ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}`. Area improved, delay still dominated by the 32:1 RAM read mux.
- **Banked corner turn** (`design4`, big win): derived the exact transpose read schedule algebraically — the y-pass at row r, beat j consumes x-output `x[r + 8·(2m+o)]` with `o = j[0]^j[2]` — and replaced four 32×32 dual-port RAMs with 8 banks of 16 entries plus a per-row wide latch. Read path became pure 2:1 muxes. Cost dropped to 4.69M.
- **2-stage carry-save multipliers + 2-cycle *181/256** (`design6`, `design7`, final): biased-operand constant multipliers (AND rows → CSA tree → registered (sum,carry) → one wrap add), and the scale-by-181/truncate-division split across two schedule beats with the truncation increment folded into the final adder. Cost 4.26M → 3.87M.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Eliminating the 32:1 RAM read decode** via the algebraic read-schedule derivation (9.5M → 4.7M path). The delay win came from moving the read off the critical path entirely.
2. **Width shrinking** (20-bit x-pass outputs proven from bound |t| < 2³⁰; 20-bit transpose words) — area.
3. **Delay-splitting the multiply and *181/256 chains** using spare schedule beats — delay 992→896ps.
4. Mod-2³² arithmetic tricks (biased operand, two's-complement correction row) that keep everything bit-exact against the original 32-bit wrap semantics.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- **Pipelined 6+6 and 8+5 split multipliers** (`design5` variants): delay didn't improve (~1120–1145ps) because each stage still contained a full 32-bit carry-propagate adder; slightly worse than design4.
- **A 16-entry flat "RAM" aliasing trick** broke double-buffering (address aliasing mod 16) — correctness failure.
- Two subtle bit-exactness bugs cost several eval cycles: a **doubled carry weight** in the CSA final add, and a **dropped row (s3) in the scale-181 tree**. Also a `[31:12]` vs `[31:11]` part-select slip. Every such bug produced confusing partial-failure patterns; one netlist-vs-RTL discrepancy was traced to a parameter-width part-select (`sum_w[31:0]` with sum_w only 30 bits → x-fill in RTL, zero-pad in Yosys) — always form sums in a fixed-width frame before part-selecting.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Algebraically deriving what a block consumes** (instead of preserving its RAM structure) removes entire mux/decode trees — the single biggest lever in a no-RAM-macro flow.
- **Arithmetic mod 2³² is a license to restructure**: CSA trees, biased operands, and correction rows all replicate wrap semantics exactly, but every step must be re-derived carefully — small sign/weight slips are silent in RTL sim until checked against golden vectors.
- Build **isolated unit testbenches** for new arithmetic blocks before integrating; the harness compiles all workspace files, so keep scratch TBs inert (I learned to gut them to comments).
- Don't trust that pipelining helps if each new stage still ends in a CPA — split at the carry-save boundary, not after it.
